First, the HWPX format uses the `hp:` namespace for all its elements, including `<hp:p>` for paragraphs and `<hp:t>` for text. While the template currently has one text run per paragraph, a robust script must be prepared for cases where placeholders might be split across multiple runs. \n\nWait, the `<hp:linesegarray>` elements are particularly important. These are layout caches used by Hancom Office to store line-breaking information. If I modify the text content—which might change the text length and line breaks—but leave these caches intact, the document will likely render incorrectly or trigger errors when opened. To ensure the document is regenerated cleanly, I must remove these `<hp:linesegarray>` nodes from any paragraph I modify.\n\nAnother consideration is the XML declaration. The template uses single quotes for the `version` and `encoding` attributes (`<?xml version='1.0' encoding='utf-8'?>`). Standard XML parsing and serialization often defaults to double quotes. To maintain maximum compatibility with the HWPX specification and avoid unnecessary file changes, I should ensure the output matches the input format as closely as possible.\n\nActually, instead of just doing a simple string replacement on the whole file, it's safer to parse the XML, iterate through the paragraphs, and specifically target the `<hp:t>` tags. This prevents accidentally replacing text in metadata or other parts of the XML that might contain similar strings. I'll use Python's `xml.etree.ElementTree` for this. I need to register the `hp` namespace so the serialization preserves the prefixes correctly.\n\nHmm, I should also consider how to track which paragraphs have actually changed. Load event data\n    with open('/root/event_data.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        event_data = json.load(f)\n\n    # Register namespace to preserve hp: prefix\n    ET.register_namespace('hp', 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML')\n\n    # Process the HWPX file\n    input_path = '/root/event_announcement_template.hwpx'\n    output_path = '/root/event_announcement_ready.hwpx'\n\n    modified_files = set()\n    all_placeholders_replaced = True\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(input_path, 'r') as zin:\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n            for item in zin.infolist():\n                data = zin.read(item.filename)\n\n                if item.filename.endswith('.xml'):\n                    try:\n                        content = data.decode('utf-8')\n                        modified_content, was_modified = process_xml_content(\n                            content, event_data, item.filename\n                        )\n\n                        if was_modified:\n                            modified_files.add(item.filename)\n                            data = modified_content.encode('utf-8')\n\n                        # Check for remaining placeholders\n                        remaining = re.findall(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', modified_content)\n                        if remaining:\n                            print(f\"WARNING: Remaining placeholders in {item.filename}: {remaining}\")\n                            all_placeholders_replaced = False\n\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        print(f\"Error processing {item.filename}: {e}\")\n\n                # Write to output preserving compression type\n                zout.writestr(item, data)\n\n    print(f\"Modified files: {modified_files}\")\n\n    # Final verification\n    print(\"\\n=== Final Verification ===\")\n    verify_output(output_path)\n\n    if all_placeholders_replaced:\n        print(\"\\nPASS: All placeholders have been replaced.\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\nFAIL: Some placeholders remain unreplaced.\")\n\n\ndef process_xml_content(content, event_data, filename):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process XML content, replacing placeholders and removing layout cache.\n    Returns (modified_content, was_modified)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Parse XML\n    root = ET.fromstring(content)\n\n    # Namespace mapping\n    ns = {'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'}\n\n    was_modified = False\n\n    # Find all paragraphs\n    for para in root.findall('.//hp:p', ns):\n        # Get all text runs in this paragraph\n        runs = para.findall('.//hp:run', ns)\n\n        if not runs:\n            continue\n\n        # Concatenate all text content from runs\n        full_text_parts = []\n        for run in runs:\n            t_elem = run.find('hp:t', ns)\n            if t_elem is not None and t_elem.text:\n                full_text_parts.append(t_elem.text)\n            else:\n                full_text_parts.append('')\n\n        full_text = ''.join(full_text_parts)\n\n        # Check if there are any placeholders\n        placeholders_found = re.findall(r'\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}', full_text)\n\n        if not placeholders_found:\n            continue\n\n        # Replace placeholders\n        new_text = full_text\n        for placeholder in placeholders_found:\n            placeholder_key = placeholder.strip()\n            if placeholder_key in event_data:\n                new_text = new_text.replace(f'{{{{{placeholder}}}}}', event_data[placeholder_key])\n                print(f\"  [{filename}] Replaced '{{{{{placeholder}}}}}' -> '{event_data[placeholder_key]}'\")\n            else:\n                print(f\"  [{filename}] WARNING: No value for placeholder '{{{{{placeholder}}}}}'\")\n\n        # Only proceed if text actually changed\n        if new_text != full_text:\n            was_modified = True\n\n            # Consolidate text into the first run and clear others\n            for i, run in enumerate(runs):\n                t_elem = run.find('hp:t', ns)\n                if t_elem is not None:\n                    if i == 0:\n                        t_elem.text = new_text\n                    else:\n                        t_elem.text = ''\n\n            # Remove layout cache (linesegarray) from modified paragraphs\n            for linesegarray in para.findall('hp:linesegarray', ns):\n                para.remove(linesegarray)\n                print(f\"  [{filename}] Removed layout cache from paragraph\")\n\n    # Convert back to string\n    # Use tostring and manually fix XML declaration to match original format\n    modified_content = ET.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8').decode('utf-8')\n\n    return modified_content, was_modified\n\n\ndef verify_output(output_path):\n    \"\"\"Verify no placeholders remain in output file\"\"\"\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'r') as z:\n        all_clear = True\n        for name in z.namelist():\n            try:\n                content = z.read(name).decode('utf-8')\n                remaining = re.findall(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', content)\n                if remaining:\n                    print(f\"FAIL: Remaining placeholders in {name}: {remaining}\")\n                    all_clear = False\n            except:\n                pass\n\n        if all_clear:\n            print(\"Verification PASSED: No remaining placeholders found.\")\n        return all_clear\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"moonshotai/kimi-k2.5-0127","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"Moonshot AI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3a86d4b6-ff58-4a11-a685-c4b54d81ce95","uuid":"bd569cb3-ad01-46f5-a320-eabc8620b506"}
